| D-326 | An Art a shown blow dooms never lights (amends D-258, D-294). While Replication shows an enemy's blow on the local fighter ahead of the server's word (its Flinch plays on the rig and is not taken back), the held Art clip freezes just short of its ActivationHold, so Replication's light, chime, FOV kick and riser never start. The word's Stagger clears the hold with nothing lit. If the blow is taken back (no word within ai.strikePrediction.confirmSeconds, or a guard or parry in it), the next rig step seeks the clip to its hold pose and the ActivationHold fires then. A release while it waits launches from the clip time it stands at (ActionLogic.syncPlan). | A blow shown before the hold pose with its word after the hold time used to play light, chime and kick on an Art the word then took away: 16 of 16 such cases. Now 0 of 24. Cost: when the server misses, the hold lights when the blow is taken back (up to 0.5 s later). | CharacterController (struckShown, waitUnlit, lightHold, Hold.unlit); tests/client/CharacterController.spec.luau. To undo: remove waitUnlit. |
| D-327 | Hit-stop holds are counted in frames (amends D-271). The Mixer picks the count once, when the hold starts, from the rig's pace: the mean of its last impact.hold.paceFrames (32) steps, leaving out any step longer than hitchFactor times their median (2.5, D-341). The count is the whole number of frames nearest the hold's length; within tieFrames (0.08) of a half frame it takes the longer count. A rig with fewer steps behind it keeps D-271's nearest-frame end.<br>Amended by D-341: hitchFactor is 2.5. | At 30 fps a Normal hit-stop (1.5 frames) held 1 frame in 44–52% of jittered runs (PC item 265(b)), and round C's tree left 1.3–2.4% of 30 Hz holds frozen past 400 frames. Now 2 frames in 100% of runs, Final 3 and Kill 5. 60 Hz stays exactly 3/6/10; 144 Hz holds 7/14/24. | impact.hold; Mixer (framesAt, holdFor, clockStep, notePace); tests/choreo/MixerHold.spec.luau. To undo: drop impact.hold and count by time. |
| D-328 | The lock key in a staged capture take acts on the staged enemy alone (extends D-290 to the F / R3 key). While TargetController.stageTarget names the take's staged enemy (DevCapture places only), toggle() follows CaptureLogic.lockPress: it locks that enemy when the lock is off or on another enemy, and lets it go when the lock is on it. It never cycles to a nearer field enemy; if the staged enemy cannot be locked, the key keeps the lock it had. stageTarget(nil) gives the key its cycle back. Inert until Capture.client calls stageTarget (open). | A player's own F in a staged take could lock a field enemy nearer than the staged one, the fault D-290 fixed for auto-perform. | TargetController (stageTarget, toggle); tests/client/TargetController.spec.luau. To undo: remove stageTarget. |

Until then the corpse's faces count in blockerClear; at offAt tickAll turns every face CanCollide off. Models already destroyed are dropped, and reset clears the list. | Since D-281 a dead enemy's faces stayed solid in the enemies group until the model was removed (combat.corpseRemoveDelay, 4 s), and blockerClear skipped corpses, so a stopped rush could turn solid over a corpse and be pushed. Now off within one tick after the shatter. | EnemyService (corpses, sweepCorpses, blockerClear); tests/server/CorpseBlocker.spec.luau. To undo: remove sweepCorpses. |

| D-331 | The validators carry the round's keys (amends D-283).<br>- combat: assistConeDegrees / 2 ≤ the snap's reach; besideHitRadius ≤ half the narrowest blade among scope.arts' weapon categories (OneHandedSword 0.32 → 0.16 against 0.15; Rapier and Shield are out of Phase 0 scope); wallGapStuds ≤ stepStuds (≤, not <: both are 0.05); besideFollowStep > besideHitRadius.<br>- camera.lock: 7 rules for the round-B keys. choreo: movingTipShare. stride: heldRiseSeconds.<br>- New tools/validators/wallSlam.luau: shakeTier is a combat.cameraShakeStuds tier, shakeRadius > 0, shakeFullRadius in [0, shakeRadius).<br>- Every new rule has a validators.spec case that fails on bad data. | Round B added these keys with no rule (its open "validator rules for the new combat, camera, fx and choreo keys"). | tools/validators/combat.luau, camera.luau, choreo.luau, stride.luau, wallSlam.luau; tests/tools/validators.spec.luau. To undo: drop the rules. |
| D-332 | Enemy spacing is face to face (amends D-259). Spacing.footprintOf takes the faces EnemyService measures at spawn (RigBuilder.footprints, EnemyService.facesOf) into front, back and radius, and keeps them. Spacing.clearance between two bodies with faces is the separation over the faces' own axes, falling back to the nearest point-to-edge distance when apart. A sliding enemy picks its way round by the sign of the cross product of its line to the goal and its offset, and keeps to that side, so it no longer swaps sides along a flat face. | Since D-281 every face meets other enemies' faces, and the kobold's axe face reaches 1.84 studs past its capsule. Closest face gaps: heap line −0.346 → 1.849, stack −0.328 → 1.063, chase ×3 −0.048 → 0.720, chase ×4 −1.823 → −0.221 (open). | Spacing (Face, Footprint.faces, faceCorners, clearance), EnemyService.facesOf; tests/server/PackFaces.spec.luau. To undo: capsule-only footprints. |
| D-333 | Studio test hooks (extends D-114). DevCombat gains "enemy", "walk", "charge", "part" and "clearparts", Studio-only through the same gate and BindableFunction, with no remote and no anti-cheat call.<br>- Positions are floor points; the root goes the rig's root height above, as a spawn's does.<br>- "enemy" (EnemyService.place) drops what the enemy was doing, pivots, halts it and makes the point its home; held if asked, otherwise its brain starts again in Idle.<br>- "walk" (EnemyService.walk) moves it at the def's moveSpeed and turn rate while the brain decides nothing; it ends within hooks.arriveStuds (1), after hooks.walkTimeoutSeconds (30), or when a hit staggers it, keeps its room to packmates, and is capped at hooks.maxWalkStuds (60).<br>- "charge" (EnemyService.charge, Charger.charge) turns the boar to face the point at once and runs Charger's normal beginAttack; it replies the launch line and the stops from a dev-only rush log, within hooks.chargeTimeoutSeconds (3).<br>- "part" makes anchored, collidable, non-touch parts in workspace.SkyspireDevParts (position + tilt or a CFrame, a name, an optional delay `after`); "clearparts" removes them and cancels pending ones. | PC runs could not aim a rush at a wall, slope or stairs, spawn or move enemies, or make enemies bump (items 241, 244, 269–272, 277 not run). A hooked charge gives the same rush line, hit, wall stop and spacing as a natural rush, and the anti-cheat recorder stays empty. | DevCombat.server, EnemyService (place, walk, walking, charge, devRushOf, engine.pivot), Charger (rushOf, charge), dev.luau hooks, tools/validators/dev.luau checkHooks; tests/server/DevCombat.spec.luau, tests/server/DevEnemyHooks.spec.luau. To undo: remove the five commands. |
| D-334 | The gait phase follows the root's real travel (amends D-291). Locomotion advances the phase by speed × dt plus how far the root really went against it: stepTravel keeps a running slip (the root's horizontal move, or its move along the followed velocity when blocked, minus speed × dt), clamped to ±choreo locomotion.stride.travelSlipSeconds (1/30 s) of travel, and each frame the phase moves by the change in that slip. The slip resets standing, in the air, on a stall frame and when held still; a teleport or shove past the bound is dropped. The root's physics position is never changed. Run and Sprint got denser stance keys and the strafes a trail-foot approach (gaitgen). Stride.spec measures on engine-like steps: a foot is flat within stride.flatBand (0.09) and may move at most stride.frameBar (0.04) a frame and slideBar (0.1) net. | PC item 282: the engine steps the root in 1/240 s substeps, 2 to 6 per rendered frame, while its velocity reads steady, so a phase clocked by render time made a planted foot hop 0.12–0.17 studs and back. A flat foot moved up to 0.258 studs between frames; now ≤ 0.037 a frame and ≤ 0.048 net per plant. | Locomotion.stepTravel, choreo.luau locomotion.stride, tools/gaitgen and the 8 regenerated gait clips; tests/choreo/Stride.spec.luau. To undo: travelSlipSeconds 0 and the old clips. |

CharacterController.onServerState stops an Art the server takes away (Stagger, GuardBroken, Break, Dead), held or launched before its first cut, over combat.struckStanceBlendOut (0.5 s, the stagger's length). The player's own ends keep combat.locomotionBlendOut (0.2 s): a dodge, a cancel, a late release, a rejected Art. combat.struckStanceTipSpeed (60 studs/s, a stud a 60 Hz frame) is the bar for that fade. To undo: struckStanceBlendOut 0.2. |
| D-341 | impact.hold.hitchFactor is 2.5 (was 1.5; amends D-327). On a vsync display that cannot hold its rate, frames last one or two refreshes (45 fps on 60 Hz is 1, 1, 2), and those two-refresh frames are the frame rate, not hitches. A real hitch, three refreshes or more, is still left out. | At 1.5 a Final froze 133 ms instead of 100 and a Kill 217–233 ms instead of 167 at 45 fps on 60 Hz; at 108 fps on 144 Hz a Final froze 125–132 ms. With 2.5: Final 5 and Kill 8 frames (100–117 and 167–183 ms); 60, 30 and 144 Hz steady and the jitter cases unchanged. | impact.luau hold.hitchFactor; tests/choreo/MixerHold.spec.luau. To undo: 1.5. |
